   129	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
   130	                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
   131	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
   132	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
   133	}
   134	
   135	void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
   136	    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   137	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
   138	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	}
   142	
   143	// Compute C = A * B on the GPU and return kernel execution time in ms.
   144	float matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A,
   145	                     const std::vector<double>& B,
   146	                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   147	    const size_t elements = N * N;
   148	    const size_t bytes = elements * sizeof(double);
   149	    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
   150	
   151	    DeviceBuffer deviceA(elements);
   152	    DeviceBuffer deviceB(elements);
   153	    DeviceBuffer deviceC(elements);
   154	
   155	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceA.get(), A.data(), bytes,
   156	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   157	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceB.get(), B.data(), bytes,
   158	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   159	
   160	    CublasHandle handle;
   161	    const double alpha = 1.0;
   162	    const double beta = 0.0;
   163	
   164	    // cuBLAS matrices are column-major. Row-major C=A*B has the same memory
   165	    // layout as column-major C^T=B^T*A^T, hence the intentionally swapped
   166	    // deviceB/deviceA arguments below.
   167	    auto launchGemm = [&]() {
   168	        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(handle.get(), CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   169	                                 dimension, dimension, dimension, &alpha,
   170	                                 deviceB.get(), dimension, deviceA.get(),
   171	                                 dimension, &beta, deviceC.get(), dimension));
   172	    };
   173	
   174	    // Initialize lazy CUDA/cuBLAS state before measuring the multiplication.
   175	    launchGemm();
   176	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   177	
   178	    CudaEvent start;
   179	    CudaEvent stop;
   180	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
   181	    launchGemm();
   182	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
   183	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
   184	
   185	    float elapsedMs = 0.0F;
   186	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start.get(), stop.get()));
   187	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(C.data(), deviceC.get(), bytes,
   188	                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   189	    return elapsedMs;
   190	}
   191	
   192	// Simple validation: compute selected elements on the CPU and compare.
   193	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A,
   194	                    const std::vector<double>& B,
   195	                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   196	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   197	
   198	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   199	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   200	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   201	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   202	
   203	            double expected = 0.0;
   204	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   205	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   206	            }
   207	
   208	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   209	            const double relError =
   210	                std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   211	
   212	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   213	                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, "
   214	                            "got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   215	                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   216	                return false;
   217	            }
   218	        }
   219	    }
   220	
   221	    return true;
   222	}
   223	
   224	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   225	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   226	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   227	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   228	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   229	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   230	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   231	}
   232	
   233	bool parseDimension(const char* text, size_t& value) {
   234	    if (text == nullptr || *text == '\0' || *text == '-') {
   235	        return false;
   236	    }
   237	
   238	    errno = 0;
   239	    char* end = nullptr;
   240	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   241	    if (errno == ERANGE || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
   242	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   243	        return false;
   244	    }
   245	
   246	    const size_t dimension = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   247	    if (dimension > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / dimension ||
   248	        dimension * dimension >
   249	            std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
   250	        return false;
   251	    }
   252	
   253	    value = dimension;
   254	    return true;
   255	}
   256	
   257	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   258	    size_t N = 512;
   259	    bool validate = false;
   260	    bool printResults = false;
   261	
   262	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   263	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   264	            if (!parseDimension(argv[++i], N)) {
   265	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Invalid matrix size: %s\n", argv[i]);
   266	                return 1;
   267	            }
   268	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   269	            validate = true;
   270	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   271	            printResults = true;
   272	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   273	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   274	            return 0;
   275	        } else {
   276	            std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   277	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   278	            return 1;
   279	        }
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   283	    std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   284	    std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   285	
   286	    const size_t elements = N * N;
   287	    std::vector<double> A(elements);
   288	    std::vector<double> B(elements);
   289	    std::vector<double> C(elements);
   290	
   291	    std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   292	    initMatrix(A, N);
   293	    initMatrix(B, N);
   294	
   295	    std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   296	    const float durationMs = matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
   297	    std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);
   298	
   299	    const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
   300	                              static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);
   301	    const double gflops = operations / (static_cast<double>(durationMs) * 1e6);
   302	    std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   303	
   304	    if (printResults) {
   305	        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    if (validate) {
   309	        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   310	        if (validateResult(A, B, C, N)) {
   311	            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   312	            return 0;
   313	        }
   314	
   315	        std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   316	        return 1;
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    return 0;
   320	}

+const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
+    switch (status) {
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";
+    }
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d for %s: %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
 }

+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t elements) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                              elements * sizeof(double)));
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    double* get() noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+class CublasHandle {
+public:
+    CublasHandle() {
+        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasCreate(&handle_));
+    }
+
+    ~CublasHandle() {
+        if (handle_ != nullptr) {
+            cublasDestroy(handle_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    CublasHandle(const CublasHandle&) = delete;
+    CublasHandle& operator=(const CublasHandle&) = delete;
+
+    cublasHandle_t get() const noexcept { return handle_; }
+
+private:
+    cublasHandle_t handle_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+class CudaEvent {
+public:
+    CudaEvent() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event_));
+    }
+
+    ~CudaEvent() {
+        if (event_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(event_);
         }
     }
+
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+
+    cudaEvent_t get() const noexcept { return event_; }
+
+private:
+    cudaEvent_t event_ = nullptr;
+};
+

- Uses optimized FP64 `cublasDgemm`, preserving row-major semantics.
- Adds CUDA-event kernel timing, GPU memory management, warm-up, and error handling.
- Updates CMake for CUDA and cuBLAS without third-party dependencies.
- Adds safe dimension validation.
- Created no new workspace files.

+const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
+    switch (status) {
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";
+    }

+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t elements) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                              elements * sizeof(double)));
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    double* get() noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    double* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+class CublasHandle {
+public:
+    CublasHandle() {
+        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasCreate(&handle_));
+    }
+
+    ~CublasHandle() {
+        if (handle_ != nullptr) {
+            cublasDestroy(handle_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    CublasHandle(const CublasHandle&) = delete;
+    CublasHandle& operator=(const CublasHandle&) = delete;
+
+    cublasHandle_t get() const noexcept { return handle_; }
+
+private:
+    cublasHandle_t handle_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+class CudaEvent {
+public:
+    CudaEvent() {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event_));
+    }
+
+    ~CudaEvent() {
+        if (event_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(event_);
         }
     }
+
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+
+    cudaEvent_t get() const noexcept { return event_; }
+
+private:
+    cudaEvent_t event_ = nullptr;
+};

+// Compute C = A * B on the GPU and return kernel execution time in ms.
+float matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                     const std::vector<double>& B,
+                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
+    const size_t elements = N * N;
+    const size_t bytes = elements * sizeof(double);
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+
+    DeviceBuffer deviceA(elements);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceB(elements);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceC(elements);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceA.get(), A.data(), bytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceB.get(), B.data(), bytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    CublasHandle handle;
+    const double alpha = 1.0;
+    const double beta = 0.0;
+
+    // cuBLAS matrices are column-major. Row-major C=A*B has the same memory
+    // layout as column-major C^T=B^T*A^T, hence the intentionally swapped
+    // deviceB/deviceA arguments below.
+    auto launchGemm = [&]() {
+        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(handle.get(), CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                 dimension, dimension, dimension, &alpha,
+                                 deviceB.get(), dimension, deviceA.get(),
+                                 dimension, &beta, deviceC.get(), dimension));
+    };
+
+    // Initialize lazy CUDA/cuBLAS state before measuring the multiplication.
+    launchGemm();
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+
+    CudaEvent start;
+    CudaEvent stop;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get()));
+    launchGemm();
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.get()));
+
+    float elapsedMs = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start.get(), stop.get()));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(C.data(), deviceC.get(), bytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    return elapsedMs;
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+
+bool parseDimension(const char* text, size_t& value) {
+    if (text == nullptr || *text == '\0' || *text == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno == ERANGE || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const size_t dimension = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    if (dimension > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / dimension ||
+        dimension * dimension >
+            std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    value = dimension;
+    return true;
 }
